[02:47:01.530] Whereas in the new system, it's heat-based.  So instead of queuing everything on the end,

[02:47:50.880] You have to make sure that stuff always executes before the other thing, like because of the graph propagation.  It was kind of a mess, but it just kind of worked.
[02:48:00.240] Whereas in the new system,
[02:48:01.920] if we don't get to processing something, we can just start the heap at the top.
